   {id:"task_demo_plt_2",title:"Maintenance inspection",owner:"Maintenance team",dueDate:"",status:"open",description:"Record field observations for management."}
  ]
 };
 let selectedSiteId="";
 function text(v,max){return String(v??"").trim().slice(0,max)}
 function safeCount(v){const n=Number(v);return Number.isSafeInteger(n)&&n>=0&&n<=999?n:0}
 function validId(v,prefix){const x=String(v||"");return new RegExp("^"+prefix+"[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,80}$").test(x)?x:""}
 function validDate(v){const x=String(v||"").trim();if(!x)return "";if(!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(x))return "";const d=new Date(x+"T00:00:00Z");return !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())&&d.toISOString().slice(0,10)===x?x:""}
 function normalizeSite(row){if(!row||typeof row!=="object")return null;const id=validId(row.id,"site_");const title=text(row.title,120);if(!id||!title)return null;const statusClass=["good","warn","bad"].includes(row.statusClass)?row.statusClass:"good";return {id,title,kind:text(row.kind,80)||"Customer job",location:text(row.location,120),crewCount:safeCount(row.crewCount),issueCount:safeCount(row.issueCount),status:text(row.status,40)||"Active",statusClass}}
 function normalizeTask(row){if(!row||typeof row!=="object")return null;const id=validId(row.id,"task_");const title=text(row.title,120),owner=text(row.owner,80),status=TASK_STATUSES.has(String(row.status||"").toLowerCase())?String(row.status).toLowerCase():"open";if(!id||!title)return null;return {id,title,owner,dueDate:validDate(row.dueDate),status,description:text(row.description,240)}}
 function localContext(){const role=String(typeof currentWorkspaceRole==="function"?currentWorkspaceRole():currentUser?.role||"");const tenantId=text(currentUser?.tenantId,128),companyId=text(store?.activeCompanyId||state?.id,128);if(!ALLOWED_ROLES.has(role)||!tenantId||!companyId)return null;return {role,tenantId,companyId}}
 async function verifiedContext(){const info=await productionApiClient.request("/api/auth/me");const user=info?.user||{};const role=String(user.role||"");const tenantId=text(user.tenantId,128),companyId=text(store?.activeCompanyId||state?.id,128);if(!ALLOWED_ROLES.has(role)||!tenantId||!companyId)throw new Error("Sites & field work is available only to an authenticated Owner or Manager.");currentUser={...(currentUser||{}),...user};if(info.csrfToken)csrfToken=info.csrfToken;return {role,tenantId,companyId}}
 function sitesKey(ctx){if(!ctx?.tenantId||!ctx?.companyId)throw new Error("tenant context required");return SITE_PREFIX+encodeURIComponent(ctx.tenantId)+":"+encodeURIComponent(ctx.companyId)}
 function tasksKey(ctx,siteId){const id=validId(siteId,"site_");if(!ctx?.tenantId||!ctx?.companyId||!id)throw new Error("canonical site context required");return TASK_PREFIX+encodeURIComponent(ctx.tenantId)+":"+encodeURIComponent(ctx.companyId)+":"+id}
 function readJson(key){try{const raw=sessionStorage.getItem(key);return raw?JSON.parse(raw):null}catch{return null}}
 function writeJson(key,value){sessionStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify(value))}
 function readSites(ctx,{seed=true}={}){if(!ctx)return [];const key=sitesKey(ctx),raw=readJson(key);let rows=Array.isArray(raw)?raw.map(normalizeSite).filter(Boolean):[];if(!rows.length&&seed){rows=DEMO_SITES.map(x=>normalizeSite(x));writeJson(key,rows)}return rows}
 function writeSites(ctx,rows){const clean=Array.isArray(rows)?rows.map(normalizeSite).filter(Boolean).slice(0,40):[];writeJson(sitesKey(ctx),clean);return clean}
 function resolveSite(ctx,siteId){const matches=readSites(ctx).filter(x=>x.id===siteId);return matches.length===1?matches[0]:null}
 function readTasks(ctx,siteId,{seed=true}={}){const site=resolveSite(ctx,siteId);if(!site)return [];const key=tasksKey(ctx,site.id),raw=readJson(key);let rows=Array.isArray(raw)?raw.map(normalizeTask).filter(Boolean).slice(0,MAX_TASKS):[];if(!rows.length&&seed){rows=(DEMO_TASKS[site.id]||[]).map(normalizeTask).filter(Boolean);writeJson(key,rows)}return rows}
 function writeTasks(ctx,siteId,rows){const site=resolveSite(ctx,siteId);if(!site)throw new Error("Canonical site could not be resolved.");const clean=Array.isArray(rows)?rows.map(normalizeTask).filter(Boolean).slice(0,MAX_TASKS):[];writeJson(tasksKey(ctx,site.id),clean);return clean}
 function statusLabel(status){return status==="done"?"Done":status==="in progress"?"In progress":"Open"}
 function setLocked(message){const list=q("#siteDemoList"),tasks=q("#siteDemoTasks"),create=q("#siteTaskCreate"),st=q("#siteDemoStatus");if(list)list.replaceChildren();if(tasks){const note=document.createElement("div");note.className="muted small";note.textContent=message;tasks.replaceChildren(note)}if(create)create.style.display="none";if(st){st.textContent="Locked";st.className="proof-state"}const sub=q("#siteDemoSubtitle");if(sub)sub.textContent=message;for(const id of ["sitesActiveCount","sitesCrewCount","sitesIssueCount","sitesTaskCount"]){const el=q("#"+id);if(el)el.textContent="—"}}
 function makeSiteButton(site,taskCount){const btn=document.createElement("button");btn.type="button";btn.className="site-demo-card"+(site.id===selectedSiteId?" active":"");btn.dataset.siteId=site.id;btn.style.width="100%";btn.style.textAlign="left";const top=document.createElement("div");top.className="between row";const name=document.createElement("b");name.textContent=site.title+" · "+site.kind;const badge=document.createElement("span");badge.className="proof-state "+site.statusClass;badge.textContent=site.status;top.append(name,badge);const sub=document.createElement("div");sub.className="muted small";sub.style.marginTop="5px";sub.textContent=(site.location||"Field site")+" · Phase 0 session";const meta=document.createElement("div");meta.className="site-demo-meta";for(const value of [site.crewCount+" crew",taskCount+" tasks",site.issueCount+" issues"]){const x=document.createElement("span");x.textContent=value;meta.appendChild(x)}btn.append(top,sub,meta);return btn}
 function renderTask(task){const row=document.createElement("div");row.className="site-demo-task";const copy=document.createElement("div"),title=document.createElement("b"),desc=document.createElement("div"),meta=document.createElement("div"),btn=document.createElement("button");title.textContent=task.title;desc.className="muted small";desc.textContent=task.description||"Site coordination task.";meta.className="muted small";meta.textContent=[task.owner||"Unassigned",task.dueDate?"Due "+task.dueDate:"No due date"].join(" · ");copy.append(title,desc,meta);btn.type="button";btn.className="btn alt small siteTaskToggle";btn.dataset.taskId=task.id;btn.textContent=statusLabel(task.status);row.append(copy,btn);return row}
 function renderCurrent(ctx=localContext()){if(!ctx){setLocked("Authenticated Owner or Manager tenant context required.");return false}let sites=readSites(ctx);if(!sites.length){setLocked("No valid site records are available in this session.");return false}if(!resolveSite(ctx,selectedSiteId))selectedSiteId=sites[0].id;const list=q("#siteDemoList");if(list){const buttons=sites.map(site=>makeSiteButton(site,readTasks(ctx,site.id).length));list.replaceChildren(...buttons)}const selected=resolveSite(ctx,selectedSiteId);if(!selected){setLocked("Selected site could not be resolved uniquely.");return false}const tasks=readTasks(ctx,selected.id);q("#siteDemoTitle").textContent=selected.title;q("#siteDemoSubtitle").textContent=(selected.location||"Field site")+" · "+selected.crewCount+" crew · "+selected.issueCount+" open issue"+(selected.issueCount===1?"":"s");const status=q("#siteDemoStatus");status.textContent=selected.status;status.className="proof-state "+selected.statusClass;const wrap=q("#siteDemoTasks");wrap.replaceChildren(...(tasks.length?tasks.map(renderTask):[Object.assign(document.createElement("div"),{className:"muted small",textContent:"No site tasks recorded in this session."})]));const create=q("#siteTaskCreate");if(create)create.style.display="";q("#sitesActiveCount").textContent=String(sites.filter(x=>!/completed/i.test(x.status)).length);q("#sitesCrewCount").textContent=String(sites.reduce((n,x)=>n+x.crewCount,0));q("#sitesIssueCount").textContent=String(sites.reduce((n,x)=>n+x.issueCount,0));q("#sitesTaskCount").textContent=String(sites.reduce((n,x)=>n+readTasks(ctx,x.id).filter(t=>t.status!=="done").length,0));const note=q("#siteTaskSecurityNote");if(note)note.textContent="Owner/Manager session verified locally; every mutation rechecks /api/auth/me before writing tenant-scoped sessionStorage.";return true}
 async function renderVerified(){try{const ctx=await verifiedContext();return renderCurrent(ctx)}catch(err){setLocked(err?.message||"Authenticated Owner or Manager tenant context required.");return false}}
 async function mutate(label,fn){try{const ctx=await verifiedContext();await fn(ctx);renderCurrent(ctx);const sr=q("#srStatus");if(sr)sr.textContent=label}catch(err){const msg=err?.message||"Sites action blocked.";const sr=q("#srStatus");if(sr)sr.textContent=msg;setLocked(msg);BW.notifications.notify(msg,{type:"warning"})}}
 async function toggleTask(taskId){await mutate("Site task updated in this browser session.",async ctx=>{const site=resolveSite(ctx,selectedSiteId);if(!site)throw new Error("Canonical site could not be resolved uniquely.");const rows=readTasks(ctx,site.id),idx=rows.findIndex(x=>x.id===taskId);if(idx<0)throw new Error("Task could not be resolved for this site.");rows[idx]={...rows[idx],status:rows[idx].status==="done"?"open":"done"};writeTasks(ctx,site.id,rows)})}
 async function addTask(){const title=text(q("#siteTaskTitle")?.value,120),owner=text(q("#siteTaskOwner")?.value,80),dueDate=validDate(q("#siteTaskDue")?.value),status=String(q("#siteTaskStatus")?.value||"open").toLowerCase();if(!title){BW.notifications.notify("Enter a task title.",{type:"warning"});q("#siteTaskTitle")?.focus();return}await mutate("Site task added to this browser session.",async ctx=>{const site=resolveSite(ctx,selectedSiteId);if(!site)throw new Error("Canonical site could not be resolved uniquely.");const rows=readTasks(ctx,site.id);if(rows.length>=MAX_TASKS)throw new Error("This demo site has reached the 200-task session limit.");const id="task_"+(globalThis.crypto?.randomUUID?.()||Date.now().toString(36)).replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9_-]/g,"");const row=normalizeTask({id,title,owner,dueDate,status,description:"Session-only site work package."});if(!row)throw new Error("Task details are invalid.");writeTasks(ctx,site.id,[...rows,row]);q("#siteTaskTitle").value="";q("#siteTaskOwner").value="";q("#siteTaskDue").value="";q("#siteTaskStatus").value="open"})}
 async function createSite(){const name=await BW.dialog.prompt({title:"Create demo site / job",message:"Phase 0 stores this site only in the authenticated browser session.",placeholder:"Site or customer job name",required:true,maxLength:120,confirmLabel:"Continue"});if(name===null)return;const location=await BW.dialog.prompt({title:"Site location",placeholder:"Town or area",maxLength:120,confirmLabel:"Create demo site"});if(location===null)return;await mutate("Demo site created in this browser session.",async ctx=>{const rows=readSites(ctx);const id="site_"+(globalThis.crypto?.randomUUID?.()||Date.now().toString(36)).replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9_-]/g,"");const site=normalizeSite({id,title:name,kind:"Customer job",location,crewCount:0,issueCount:0,status:"Active",statusClass:"good"});if(!site)throw new Error("Site details are invalid.");writeSites(ctx,[...rows,site]);selectedSiteId=site.id;writeTasks(ctx,site.id,[])})}

<script id="v82-sidebar-accessibility-js">
(function(){
  function esc(value){return window.CSS&&typeof CSS.escape==='function'?CSS.escape(String(value||'')):String(value||'').replace(/(["\\])/g,'\\$1')}
  function openGroupForView(view){const btn=document.querySelector('#nav button[data-view="'+esc(view)+'"]');const group=btn?.closest('details.nav-access-group,details.nav-specialist-tools');if(group)group.open=true}
  function syncGroupVisibility(){document.querySelectorAll('#nav details.nav-access-group').forEach(group=>{const visible=[...group.querySelectorAll('button[data-view]')].filter(b=>b.style.display!=="none");group.style.display=visible.length?"":"none";const count=group.querySelector('.nav-access-count');if(count)count.textContent=String(visible.length)});const more=document.querySelector('#nav .nav-specialist-tools');if(more){const count=more.querySelector('.nav-specialist-count');if(count)count.textContent='3';more.dataset.totalTools='3'}}
  const priorShow=window.showView;if(typeof priorShow==='function')window.showView=function(id){const out=priorShow.apply(this,arguments);if(out)openGroupForView(id);setTimeout(syncGroupVisibility,0);return out};
  window.whenThebeWorkspaceReady(()=>{syncGroupVisibility();const active=document.querySelector('#nav button[data-view].active');if(active)openGroupForView(active.dataset.view);setTimeout(syncGroupVisibility,100)});
})();
</script>

<script id="thebe-mobile-experience-runtime">
(function mobileExperienceV83(){
  "use strict";
  const MOBILE_MAX=760;
  const isMobile=()=>window.matchMedia(`(max-width:${MOBILE_MAX}px)`).matches;
  function sidebar(){return document.getElementById("workspaceSidebar")}
  function menuButton(){return document.getElementById("mobileMenuButton")}
  function ensureChrome(){
    const side=sidebar(); if(!side)return;
    if(!document.getElementById("mobileNavClose")){
      const close=document.createElement("button");
      close.id="mobileNavClose";close.className="mobile-nav-close";close.type="button";
      close.setAttribute("aria-label","Close workspace menu");close.textContent="×";
      close.addEventListener("click",closeMobileWorkspaceMenu);
      side.appendChild(close);
    }
    if(!document.getElementById("mobileNavBackdrop")){
      const back=document.createElement("div");back.id="mobileNavBackdrop";back.className="mobile-nav-backdrop";back.setAttribute("aria-hidden","true");
      back.addEventListener("click",closeMobileWorkspaceMenu);document.body.appendChild(back);
    }
  }
  function openMobileWorkspaceMenu(){
    if(!isMobile()){ if(typeof window.openCommandPalette==="function")window.openCommandPalette(); return; }
    ensureChrome();document.body.classList.add("mobile-nav-open");
    menuButton()?.setAttribute("aria-expanded","true");
    setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById("mobileNavClose")?.focus(),0);
  }
  function closeMobileWorkspaceMenu(){
    document.body.classList.remove("mobile-nav-open");menuButton()?.setAttribute("aria-expanded","false");
  }
  window.openMobileWorkspaceMenu=openMobileWorkspaceMenu;
  window.closeMobileWorkspaceMenu=closeMobileWorkspaceMenu;

  function syncMobileActive(view){
    document.querySelectorAll("#mobileBar [data-mobile-view]").forEach(btn=>btn.classList.toggle("active",btn.dataset.mobileView===view));
  }
  function bindNavigation(){
    sidebar()?.querySelectorAll("button[data-view]").forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener("click",()=>{syncMobileActive(btn.dataset.view);if(isMobile())closeMobileWorkspaceMenu()}));
    document.querySelectorAll(".nav-access-group").forEach(group=>group.addEventListener("toggle",()=>{
      if(!isMobile()||!group.open)return;
      document.querySelectorAll(".nav-access-group").forEach(other=>{if(other!==group)other.open=false});
    }));
  }
  function install(){ensureChrome();bindNavigation();syncMobileActive("dashboard")}
  window.whenThebeWorkspaceReady(install);
  document.addEventListener("keydown",event=>{if(event.key==="Escape"&&document.body.classList.contains("mobile-nav-open"))closeMobileWorkspaceMenu()});
  window.addEventListener("resize",()=>{if(!isMobile())closeMobileWorkspaceMenu()},{passive:true});
  window.addEventListener("orientationchange",closeMobileWorkspaceMenu,{passive:true});
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
